Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-2, 0, 2)
find y: int(-2, 0, 2)
find z: int(-2, 0, 2)
find small_xy: bool

such that

sum([sum([product([2,x;int(1..)]),product([2,y;int(1..)]);int(1..)]),product([2,z;int(1..)]);int(1..)]) >= 0,
(small_xy) <-> (sum([product([3,x;int(1..)]),product([2,y;int(1..)]);int(1..)]) <= 0)

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#rank_1: bool
  find z#rank_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
